( 4 ) the interaction in brain spectrin is ~ 60 - fold stronger than in the erythroid counterpart, but no further enhancement is detected on adding three further pairs of repeats. ( 5 ) formation of a tight αiβi dimer likely depends on structural coupling of the first two repeats in each chain. these findings, along with the sequence analysis of the strongly interacting repeats, provide a basis for understanding the different properties of the two spectrin isoforms and suggest the evolutionary path by which the erythroid and non - erythroid sequences diverged.
